The five latest online A/B evaluations across different business settings reported gains including 10-15% in acquisition efficiency, 15-20% in target-segment advertising spend, and 0.3-0.8% in watch time; the watch-time model used approximately 10% fewer FLOPs and parameters. A dependency-aware historical-replay benchmark further evaluates research allocation, with initial results showing no consistent efficiency gain from more complex scheduling when agents already analyze and select concrete candidates.

</details>


### [95] [Synthetic Hospital: An Open, Verifiable, Physician-Validated Longitudinal EHR Benchmark](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30027)
*Christine Park,Valerie Chen,Tim Dettmers*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 Synthetic Hospital，一个开放、全合成、事实有据、纵向的电子病历（EHR）基准，解决了真实 EHR 数据因隐私无法共享且缺乏可验证真值的问题；在盲评中医生仅以接近随机（53%）的准确率区分其与真实病历，且前沿模型表现远未达上限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 前沿语言模型很少被用于临床工作流，原因是开发所需的高质量纵向基准稀缺：真实 EHR 数据因隐私、伦理和数据使用限制无法公开共享，且病历只记录临床医生的书写内容、不包含可验证的真值（ground truth）。因此需要一种既能开放共享、又具备可验证真值的纵向 EHR 基准。

Method: 作者构建了 Synthetic Hospital，其全部数据来源于公开的医学教育材料（不含任何受保护的健康信息），包含 1,268 名纵向患者和 5,602 次就诊。每个诊断、发现和时间关系都锚定在标准本体（ICD-10-CM、SNOMED CT、LOINC）上，并保留回溯到原始医学教育材料的完整溯源链。该基准通过一个模拟医院记录系统提供，该系统模拟真实 EHR 基础设施，包括标准互操作 API、基于角色的访问控制以及函数调用接口。评估通过盲评（医生区分真假记录）和 10 个前沿及开源模型的性能测试进行。

Result: 在盲评中，医生区分合成记录与真实患者图表的准确率仅为 53%，接近随机水平。在 10 个前沿与开源模型中，无一接近性能上限：最佳模型重建患者纵向问题列表的严重程度加权 F1 为 0.73，与七位医生在匹配子集上的平均水平相当，但显著低于医生中最佳表现（0.89）；在总结病历时，模型漏掉约一半的临床相关发现。

Conclusion: Synthetic Hospital 是一个困难且真实的临床 AI 性能测试基准：其合成记录足以以假乱真（医生难以分辨），同时暴露出当前前沿模型在纵向问题列表重建和病历总结上的明显不足，为临床 AI 的开发与评估提供了开放、可复现且真值可验证的平台。

Abstract: Frontier language models are rarely used in clinical workflows because the realistic, longitudinal benchmarks needed to develop them are scarce. Real electronic health record (EHR) data cannot be openly shared due to privacy, ethics or data use issues and it does not contain verifiable ground truth since the chart records only reflect what clinicians documented. We introduce Synthetic Hospital, an open, fully synthetic, fact-grounded longitudinal EHR benchmark that resolves the open sharing and verifiable ground truth barriers. Built entirely from public medical-education material with no protected health information, it comprises 1,268 longitudinal patients and 5,602 encounters, where every diagnosis, finding, and temporal relation is grounded in standard ontologies (ICD-10-CM, SNOMED CT, LOINC) and with a complete provenance chain back to its source medical education material. Synthetic Hospital is served through a simulated hospital record system that mirrors real EHR infrastructure (standard interoperability APIs, role-based access and function-calling interface). In a blinded review, physicians distinguished its records from real patient charts at near-chance rates (53\%). Across 10 frontier and open models, none approaches ceiling: the best model reconstructs a patient's longitudinal problem list with a severity-weighted F1 of 0.73, level with the mean of seven physicians on a matched subset but well below the best of them (0.89), and misses roughly half of clinically relevant findings when summarizing a chart. Overall, these results highlight that Synthetic Hospital is a difficult and realistic test of clinical AI performance.

</details>


### [96] [How does Adversarial Influence Scale in Multi-Agent Systems?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30028)
*Addison J. Wu,Jasin Cekinmez,Michel Liao,Karthik Narasimhan,Thomas L. Griffiths*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 多智能体协商虽能提升性能，但在存在恶意欺骗者时，群体易受欺骗的程度不取决于智能体总数，而取决于欺骗者所占比例；初始正确智能体反转为错误答案的叛变率随该比例线性上升，且大语言模型智能体在欺骗者为少数时仍会叛变，与人类从众行为显著不同。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多智能体协作假设所有参与者诚实，但现实中某些智能体可能因自身目标或外部指令而具有欺骗性、暗中破坏群体决策。因此需要研究协作群组在增大规模、提高欺骗者占比时，对欺骗的易感性与脆弱性如何变化。

Method: 通过多智能体协商实验，系统改变群体规模与欺骗者比例，测量初始正确智能体转为错误最终答案的叛变率；对比人类从众研究，并考察不同模型组合（尤其是诚实方模型）以及欺骗者私下协调对欺骗效果的影响。

Result: 叛变率随欺骗者比例线性上升，与群体总规模无关；与人类只有在误导者占多数时才被左右不同，LLM智能体在欺骗者仍为少数时即频繁叛变；易感性还取决于交互的模型组合；欺骗者私下协调反而可能降低其有效性。

Conclusion: 仅靠增加智能体数量不足以抵御欺骗，因为攻击者可随群体规模同步扩展；多智能体系统的健壮性不能依赖规模效应，需另寻结构性防御手段。

Abstract: Multi-agent deliberation can improve performance, but what happens when some agents do not act in good faith? In practice, an agent may be deceptive and work to subvert the group, whether through its own objectives or external instruction. We study how susceptibility to deception scales as groups increase in size and deceivers become more prevalent. It is not the number of agents in the group that matters, but the proportion of deceivers. We observe that the defection rate, how often initially correct agents switch to an incorrect final answer, rises linearly with this proportion. Whereas humans in comparable conformity studies are reliably swayed only when misleading confederates form a majority, LLM agents defect regularly even when deceivers remain a minority. Susceptibility also depends on which models are interacting, especially on the honest agent side. Unexpectedly, allowing deceivers to coordinate privately can make them less effective. Altogether, our results show that adding more agents is therefore not a sufficient defense, because the adversary can simply scale with the group.

</details>


### [97] [Style, Not Self: Surface Cues Explain Zero-Shot Code Attribution by Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30048)
*Ehsan Barkhordar,Surendrabikram Thapa*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文测试了当前商用大语言模型能否"认出"自己生成的代码，发现模型在零样本下的自识别能力接近随机水平，观测到的自我偏好主要由代码长度等表面特征驱动，经规则化归一后大多消失。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究"语言模型能否识别自己写的代码"这一假设：若模型能认出自己的代码，可能在评估（当裁判）时偏向自己，或同类模型的互相监督会发生串通，从而威胁AI安全与评估的公正性。

Method: 采用零样本测试，让多个商用LLM生成MBPP、HumanEval、DS-1000等基准的解答，并在四个任务中充当评估者：从一对解答中挑出自己的、判断单个解答是否为自己所写、识别两个解答中哪个由指定模型生成、以及盲评质量。并引入基于规则的归一化（剥离docstring、注释、类型提示和局部变量名），比较归一化前后的表现，同时用训练过的分类器做对照。

Result: 单解答任务中，15个模型-基准组合的平衡准确率仅为49-58%，原始准确率（38-67%）主要反映模型声称"归我所有"的倾向。成对任务中，14个评估者-对手组合的准确率与评估者解答更长的频率相关（r=0.93）。命名归属任务部分组合成功、部分稳定反转。规则归一化保持Pass@1不变，且12个复测结果中10个降至随机水平，其余2个仅跟随残余长度差，但训练过的分类器仍能分离多数归一化后的组合；Claude Haiku的自我偏好也消失。

Conclusion: 当前商用模型零样本下无法可靠识别自己的代码，表面上的自我偏好主要来自长度等浅层特征而非真正的"自我识别"。建议在相关研究中报告平衡准确率、启发式基线（如长度）和标签一致性。

Abstract: If a language model can recognize code it wrote, it may favor that code as a judge, and instances of one model monitoring each other could collude. We test this zero-shot on current commercial models. Five LLMs generate solutions to MBPP, HumanEval, and DS-1000, seven more to MBPP, and models act as evaluators in four tasks: picking their own solution from a pair, judging whether a single solution is their own, identifying which of two solutions a named model wrote, and judging quality blind. In the single-solution task, balanced accuracy is 49-58% for all 15 model-benchmark combinations, while raw accuracy (38-67%) mostly reflects how readily a model claims authorship. In the pairwise task, accuracy across 14 evaluator-opponent combinations correlates at r=0.93 with how often the evaluator's solution is longer. Attribution to a named model succeeds on some pairs and is consistently inverted on others. A rule-based normalization that strips docstrings, comments, type hints, and local names preserves Pass@1 and leaves ten of twelve re-tested results at chance; the other two follow a length difference it leaves, although a trained classifier still separates most normalized pairs. Claude Haiku's self-preference also disappears. We recommend reporting balanced accuracy, heuristic baselines, and label consistency.

</details>


### [98] [NNV3: Expanding Neural Network Verification to New Architectures and Domains](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30050)
*Anne M. Tumlin,Samuel Sasaki,Ben Wooding,Diego Manzanas Lopez,Muhammad Usama Zubair,Navid Hashemi,Hongchao Zhang,Waseem Abbas,Ipek Oguz,Meiyi Ma,Taylor T. Johnson*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: NNV3 是 NNV 工具的最新版本，在原有集合可达性基础上，新增 ModelStar、VolumeStar、GraphStar 等 Star 集家族成员，并引入概率可达性、公平性验证及多个新基准，成为覆盖多种 AI 系统的更全面、稳健、易用的 MATLAB 形式化验证框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着深度学习模型和基于学习的信息物理系统日益复杂多样（如权重扰动、视频/体数据、图神经网络等），现有 NNV 1.0 与 2.0 在可验证模型类型、输入形态及确定性验证能力上存在不足，亟需一个能覆盖更多 AI 系统、兼顾严格性与可扩展性的统一验证框架。

Method: 在 NNV 1.0（FFNN、CNN、NNCS）与 NNV 2.0（RNN、SSNN、Neural ODE）的集合可达性基础上，扩展 Star 集家族：提出 ModelStar（处理权重扰动）、VolumeStar（处理视频与 3D 体输入）、GraphStar（处理图神经网络）；引入基于共形推断的概率可达性模式以应对确定性验证不可解的问题；并推出 FairNNV 对连续输入区域上的反事实与个体公平性进行认证，同时配套新基准与统一文档站点。

Result: NNV3 实现了对带权重扰动网络、视频/3D 体输入、图神经网络等形式化验证的原生支持，借助概率可达性扩展了可验证问题的范围，并能够认证反事实与个体公平性属性；新增了恶意软件检测、图式电力系统模型、医学影像、变长时间序列与动作识别等基准，显著提升了工具的覆盖面与易用性。

Conclusion: NNV3 标志着 NNV 从专用验证工具发展为面向多种 AI 系统的全面、稳健且易用的形式化验证框架，这些重大更新进一步巩固了其在深度学习模型与学习使能信息物理系统验证领域的实用价值。

Abstract: We present NNV3, the latest version of the Neural Network Verification (NNV) tool, a MATLAB framework for formal verification of deep learning models and learning-enabled cyber-physical systems. Building on the set-based reachability foundation of NNV 1.0 (FFNNs, CNNs, NNCS) and NNV 2.0 (RNNs, SSNNs, neural ODEs), NNV3 introduces new members of the Star-set family: ModelStar for verifying networks under weight perturbation, VolumeStar for video and 3D volumetric inputs, and GraphStar for graph neural networks. A conformal-inference-based probabilistic reachability mode complements sound analysis for problems where deterministic verification is intractable, while FairNNV certifies counterfactual and individual fairness properties over continuous input regions. NNV3 introduces new benchmarks for malware detection, graph-based power-system models, medical imaging, variable-length time series data, and action recognition. NNV3 also incorporates tutorials and developer guides through a unified documentation site. This paper details these major updates, demonstrating NNV's maturation into a comprehensive, robust, and accessible verification tool for a diverse range of AI systems.

</details>


### [99] [SciWalker: Synthesizing Scientific Coding Problems with Operator Graphs and Execution Feedback](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30054)
*Chenxi Li,Wenxuan Zeng,Yun Luo,Fangchen Yu,Peng Ye,Yu Cheng,Jun Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 针对科学编码训练数据稀缺的问题，本文提出 SciWalker 框架，通过算子链采样与执行反馈合成高质量科学编程问题，构建了 8,178 道跨 5 个科学领域的问题，并用其对 Qwen3.5-9B 进行强化学习，使 SciCode 子问题准确率提升 9.9 个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 提升 LLM 科学编码能力需要高质量训练数据，但现有数据稀缺：人工编写真实科学问题成本高、耗时长，且难以系统性覆盖多样科学领域与算法组合。因此需要一个可扩展、具科学基础且可执行的自动合成框架。

Method: 提出 SciWalker 框架：将科学库接口与操作模式结合以实例化算子，组织为算子图，并采样算子链作为计算工作流线索；以这些线索引导 LLM 生成科学问题陈述、参考解与测试用例，失败的生成通过执行反馈迭代修复；结合结构化工作流组合、验证与质量审查实现可扩展的任务生成。

Result: 利用该框架构建了 8,178 道高质量题目，覆盖 5 个科学领域与 32 个子领域。使用 GSPO 算法对 Qwen3.5-9B 进行强化学习后，SciCode 子问题准确率从 29.3% 提升至 39.2%（+9.9 个百分点），并在科学代码生成、代码修复与推理基准上均有提升。

Conclusion: SciWalker 通过结构化工作流组合与执行反馈验证，实现了可扩展且具科学基础、计算多样性与可执行性的任务合成，所合成数据对提升 LLM 科学编码能力具有实际的训练价值。

Abstract: Improving the scientific coding capabilities of large language models (LLMs) requires high-quality training data. However, such data remain scarce because manually authoring realistic problems is costly and time-consuming, while systematically covering diverse scientific domains and algorithmic combinations remains challenging. To address this, we introduce SciWalker, a framework for synthesizing scientific coding problems through operator-chain sampling and execution feedback. The framework combines scientific library interfaces with operation modes to instantiate operators, organizes them into operator graphs, and samples operator chains as computational workflow cues. Guided by these cues, we adopt LLMs to generate scientifically grounded problem statements, reference solutions, and tests, with failed generations iteratively repaired using execution feedback. By combining structured workflow composition with verification and quality review, SciWalker enables scalable task generation while promoting scientific grounding, computational diversity, and executability. Using this framework, we construct 8,178 high-quality problems spanning 5 scientific domains and 32 subdomains. To evaluate their training utility, we conduct reinforcement learning on Qwen3.5-9B using the GSPO algorithm. This training improves SciCode subproblem accuracy by 9.9 percentage points, from 29.3% to 39.2%, with gains across scientific code generation, code repair, and reasoning benchmarks. The code for SciWalker is available at https://github.com/lichenx1/SciWalker.

</details>


### [100] [Self-Play Pretraining with Zero Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30063)
*Aditya Cowsik,Kfir Dolev,Michael Y. Li,G. Bruno De Luca,Nourya Cohen,Noah D. Goodman,Yoav Levine*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出"零数据自博弈预训练"（Self-Play Pretraining with Zero Data），让生成器与学习器在通用图灵机程序空间上自博弈，由生成器为学习器生成"能力前沿"的合成数据，实现无需人类数据、仅受算力限制的自举式预训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有语言建模的扩展主要依赖为模型预先人工策展的大量数据，训练数据仍受限于人类知识。更通用的预训练范式应让模型学会自己生成最有用的训练数据，从而获得理论上是无限的、受算力而非人类知识限制的数据来源。

Method: 受所罗门诺夫归纳法启发，将合成数据生成建模为对所有可计算结构空间的搜索。两个模型从随机初始化开始协同学习：生成器提出由通用图灵机解释的程序并产出字节序列，学习器自回归地预测这些字节序列。学习器用标准交叉熵训练；生成器用强化学习训练，目标是产出处于学习器能力前沿的序列，形成自适应课程。通用图灵机提供了覆盖所有可计算数据生成过程的搜索空间。

Result: 在多个自然数据集上，零样本损失随自博弈算力呈现可预测的扩展规律（scaling）。模型还表现出上下文学习（in-context learning）能力，并在训练过程中自发发现了可识别的数学序列。

Conclusion: 该工作作为概念验证，证明了"零数据自博弈预训练"的可行性：无需自然数据即可通过自博弈获得可迁移的通用能力，且性能随算力可预测地扩展，为实现以算力为限的通用预训练奠定了初步基础。

Abstract: Advances in language modeling have been driven by scaling pretraining on ever more data. Yet, the training data is still largely curated on the model's behalf. A more general approach to pretraining would let the model learn to generate the data most useful for its own improvement. This would provide an effectively unbounded source of training data, limited by compute rather than human knowledge. We introduce Self-Play Pretraining with Zero Data, an initial proof-of-concept towards realizing this vision. Our procedure casts synthetic data generation as a search over the space of all computable structure, taking inspiration from Solomonoff induction. Starting from random initialization, two models learn in tandem: a generator proposes programs interpreted by a universal Turing machine, generating byte sequences, while a learner autoregressively predicts these byte sequences. The learner is trained with standard cross-entropy, while the generator is trained with reinforcement learning to produce sequences at the frontier of the learner's capabilities, yielding an adaptive curriculum. A universal Turing machine gives us a search space over all computable data-generating processes, imposing little domain-specific structure, and self-play searches over this space for useful training data. We test whether zero-shot performance on natural data improves predictably with self-play compute; this is a clean test of transfer since neither generator nor learner is trained on natural data. Across several natural datasets, zero-shot loss exhibits predictable scaling in compute. The models also exhibit in-context learning, and discover recognizable mathematical sequences during training.

</details>


### [101] [PrivDrift: Auditing User-Secret Leakage Under Topic Drift in Active LLM Conversations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30094)
*Luciano Maldonado*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 PriveDrift 基准，用于审计大模型中用户泄露的秘密在会话话题漂移后是否仍可被提取，结果显示泄漏率高达 38.7%–54.6%，且额外话题漂移并不能可靠降低风险。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM 越来越多地作为持久化助手运行在面向用户、共享会话和工具增强场景中；当用户在对话中透露敏感信息后，即使话题转向无关内容，该信息仍可能在后续提示中被行为层面恢复。现有隐私研究多聚焦训练数据记忆或即时越狱，缺乏对“活跃上下文中的持续行为性隐私失败”的系统评估。

Method: 构建 PriveDrift 基准，包含 1,000 个受控多轮对话，植入种子秘密、内容密集的话题漂移轮次以及标准化的提取探针；在三个具备扩展上下文窗口的大模型上，测量对话级混合泄漏率，并分析其随模型、秘密类型和说服强度的变化，以及漂移窗口内额外漂移对泄漏的影响。

Result: 三个 LLM 的对话级混合泄漏率介于 38.7% 至 54.6%，且随模型、秘密类型和说服强度显著变化；在测试的漂移窗口内，额外话题漂移并不能可靠地降低泄漏率。

Conclusion: 活跃 LLM 上下文中的隐私风险应被评估为一种持续性的行为失败模式，而不仅仅局限于训练数据记忆或即时越狱行为。

Abstract: Large language models increasingly operate as persistent assistants in user-facing, shared-session, and tool-augmented settings. When users disclose sensitive information during an active conversation, that information may remain behaviorally recoverable through later prompts even after the dialogue shifts to unrelated topics. We introduce \textbf{PrivDrift}, a benchmark for auditing whether user-disclosed secrets remain recoverable after conversational topic drift and persuasion-based probing. PrivDrift contains 1{,}000 controlled multi-turn dialogues with seeded secrets, content-dense drift turns, and standardized extraction probes. Across three LLMs with extended context windows, dialogue-level hybrid leakage remains substantial, ranging from 38.7\% to 54.6\%, and varies strongly by model, secret type, and persuasion intensity. Within the tested drift window, additional topic drift does not reliably reduce leakage, suggesting that privacy risk in active LLM contexts should be evaluated as a persistent behavioral failure mode rather than only as training-data memorization or immediate jailbreak behavior.

</details>


### [102] [HEXIS: Compiling Skills into Extended Finite State Machines](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30123)
*Minghao LI*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 HEXIS，一种将 Agent 技能编译为扩展有限状态机（EFSM）的增量编译器，将技能知识与控制流分离以提升智能体任务执行的正确性与效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 Agent 技能虽提供可复用的知识与指令，但智能体仍需反复推断如何应用技能以及后续应执行何种操作，导致任务推理与控制决策耦合，可能遗漏或错误执行规定的步骤。

Method: HEXIS 将 Agent 技能编译为扩展有限状态机（EFSM），实现知识与控制流分离：技能知识被纳入局部指令，在状态内引导推理与生成；状态机记录执行进度与中间结果，并由显式的转移条件决定后续操作。其增量编译器首先将技能子句与工具接口映射为状态操作、局部指令、数据绑定和转移；随后将开发轨迹与现有状态对齐，识别缺失的操作与依赖，通过新增或复用状态并细化连接来吸收这些内容；更新仅在通过静态检查并回放当前及所有历史已接受轨迹后才被采纳。

Result: 在四个基准测试和四种执行器上，HEXIS 相较 Skill + ReAct 平均将成功率提升 16.1 个百分点；Qwen3.8-27B 在各基准上的执行 token 数减少 38.4% 至 88.9%。

Conclusion: HEXIS 通过将技能编译为扩展有限状态机、用增量编译与轨迹回放保证更新正确性，有效分离了技能知识与控制决策，在提升任务成功率的同时显著降低了执行成本。

Abstract: Agent skills provide reusable knowledge and instructions, yet agents must repeatedly infer how to apply them and which operation should follow. This couples task reasoning with control decisions, allowing prescribed steps to be omitted or applied incorrectly. We introduce HEXIS, which compiles agent skills into extended finite state machines that separate knowledge from control flow. Skill knowledge is incorporated into local instructions that guide reasoning and generation within states. The machine records execution progress and intermediate results, while explicit transition conditions determine subsequent operations. Our incremental compiler first maps skill clauses and tool interfaces to state operations, local instructions, data bindings, and transitions. It then aligns development traces with existing states to identify missing operations and dependencies. These are incorporated by adding or reusing states and refining their connections. Updates are accepted only after static checks and replay of the current and all previously accepted traces. Across four benchmarks and four executors, HEXIS improves success over Skill + ReAct by 16.1 percentage points on average. Qwen3.8-27B reduces execution tokens by 38.4-88.9% across benchmarks.

</details>


### [103] [Screen Before You Serve: Simulation for Production Customer Experience AI Agents at 140M Scale](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30137)
*Edesio Alcoba,Kevin Rossell,Aman Gupta,Shao Tang,Jiwoo Hong,Pabel Carrillo-Mendoza,Wanderson Conceição Ferreira,Alvaro Tedeschi,Zayd Simjee,Shreya Rajpal,Bruno Finardi Hime,Christian Sousa,Luis Moneda,Herbert Fei,Daniel Silva,Rohan Ramanath*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 Snowglobe，一个基于假设驱动的模拟工作流，用于在部署前筛选客户体验（CX）智能体。通过在 Nubank 的 Card Delivery 和 Card Management 两个客服智能体上的应用，证明模拟评估与生产表现高度相关，并借助模拟驱动的迭代显著提升了 tNPS 与自助服务率（SSR）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 提升受监管行业中的 CX 智能体十分困难：它们需要识别用户意图、遵循复杂运营策略并可靠使用工具。传统人工端到端测试覆盖面有限，而线上实验会让真实客户暴露于失败风险，损害信任。因此需要一种无需触碰生产后端、也不会影响客户的评估与筛选手段。

Method: 提出假设驱动的模拟工作流 Snowglobe：合成客户对智能体回复作出反应，并模拟工具输出以支持多步智能体流程而不调用生产后端。通过该模拟器在 Nubank 的 Card Delivery 及其扩展版 Card Management（巴西最高频的聊天客服智能体）上筛选候选版本，开展超过 16,000 次模拟对话，并对 4 个已部署版本进行模拟与生产的版本级二元评估器分数相关性验证。

Result: 在 4 个已部署版本上，模拟与生产的版本级二元评估器分数呈现高相关性；模拟驱动的迭代使线上 A/B 测试中的交易性净推荐值（tNPS）提升 36.69 分；通过模拟筛选出的开源权重配置，在后续线上 A/B 测试中将自助服务率（SSR）提升 8.82 个百分点，达到 Nubank 历史最高水平，且 tNPS 无统计学显著变化。

Conclusion: Snowglobe 模拟使模型、推理设置和提示词的广泛探索成为可能，且无需让客户承担风险，从而实现了仅靠线上实验难以达成的生产改进。该方法为受监管行业中 CX 智能体的安全部署前筛选提供了一条可行路径。

Abstract: Customer experience (CX) agents use tools and large language models to address customer requests and guide conversational interactions with an organization's products. Improving these agents, especially in regulated industries, is difficult: they must detect intent, follow complex operational policies and use tools reliably. Manual end-to-end testing offers limited coverage, while live experiments expose customers to failures that can erode trust.
  We present a hypothesis-driven simulation workflow for screening candidate CX agents before deployment. Synthetic customers react to agent responses and simulated tool outputs enable multi-step agentic workflows without invoking production backends. We use the Snowglobe simulator on Nubank's Card Delivery agent and its expanded successor, Card Management - Nubank's highest-volume chat-support agent in Brazil. Across 4 deployed versions, simulated and production version-level binary evaluator scores show high correlation. Simulation-guided iteration increased transactional net promoter score (tNPS) by 36.69 points in a live A/B test. We also screened open-weight configurations in over 16,000 simulated conversations. In a subsequent live A/B test, the selected model increased self-service rate (SSR) by 8.82 percentage points to the highest level observed at Nubank, with no statistically significant change in tNPS. Simulation made broad exploration of models, reasoning settings, and prompts feasible without customer exposure, enabling production improvements that would have been impractical to pursue through live experimentation alone.

</details>


### [104] [EnigmaForge: The Question Is Hidden in the Story](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30144)
*Daniel Eisner*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: EnigmaForge 不直接向模型提问，而是把逻辑谜题藏进一叠旧文档（信件、收据、日志）中，测试模型能否仅凭"故事"推断出任务并求解。它在生成阶段用 SAT 求解器保证解唯一，并用消融证书证明每条线索都不可缺失。该基准可无限生成新实例，以"直觉"（仅凭故事完成任务）为核心指标、世界重建为次级指标，在 25 个前沿模型、600+ 实例上刷新了排行榜。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准大多显式给出问题，无法区分模型"会解题"与"能自主发现问题并重建世界"的能力；同时，静态收集的语料存在饱和风险。作者希望构建一种不可穷尽、且能衡量模型"直觉式理解"的评估方式。

Method: EnigmaForge 采用程序化生成：把一个小型逻辑谜题的线索散布于信件、收据和日志边注等旧文档中，不附带任何显式问题；生成时用 SAT 求解器证明解唯一，并输出消融证书验证每条线索都是必要（load-bearing）的。评估设置三种配对条件，对 25 个前沿模型在 600+ 实例（共 17,400 条评分记录）上测试，以"仅凭故事即完成任务"的直觉分数为主指标，世界重建为次级指标。

Result: 在"直觉"维度上模型表现出现 22 倍差距，而事实恢复能力仅 1.6 倍差距；事实恢复第二名的模型在直觉榜上仅排第十四；存在一个模型对"是否被告知问题"表现无差异，另有一个模型在"不给问题"时反而显著更优。此外，多个模型在到达谜题前就被自身内容过滤器拦截——任何把拒绝回答计为失败的评分方式，实际上都在静默测量过滤器的行为。

Conclusion: EnigmaForge 揭示了现有模型在显式提问下表现良好、却在仅凭语境自主发现并求解问题时能力差异被大幅放大；"直觉"作为评估轴能重排排行榜。同时提醒基准设计者：拒绝回答不应简单计为失败，否则会混淆模型能力与安全过滤行为。

Abstract: Most benchmarks hand the model a question. EnigmaForge hands it a stack of old documents and no question at all. Buried in the letters, receipts, and logbook margins is a small logic puzzle whose solution is unique - proved by a SAT solver at generation time, with an ablation certificate showing every clue is load-bearing. Because instances are generated rather than collected, the corpus renews forever. The headline measure is intuition: task success when handed only the story, with world reconstruction as the secondary axis. Twenty-five frontier models ran over 600 instances (17,400 scored records) under three matched conditions. Intuition reshuffles the leaderboard: a 22x spread where fact recovery spans 1.6x, the second-best fact-recoverer ranks fourteenth, one model is indifferent to being told the question, and another is significantly better without it. Several models were blocked by their own content filters before reaching the puzzle - any benchmark scoring refusals as failure is quietly measuring filter behavior.

</details>


### [105] [GRASP: Generating, Revising, and Assessing for Strategic Planning with Agentic AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30147)
*Arunabh Srivastava,Mohammad A.,Khojastepour,Srimat Chakradhar,Sennur Ulukus*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: GRASP 是一个策略感知的多阶段规划框架，通过隔离上下文的模块化设计（GenPlan/RevPlan/VerPlan），让 LLM 在多任务复杂规划中持续取得 SOTA，并几乎消除多任务扩展带来的性能下降。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM 的性能随任务复杂度上升而可靠性下降；尤其在多任务扩展场景下，直接 LLM 规划器会出现性能急剧崩溃，难以稳定生成高质量的自然语言可执行计划。

Method: 提出 GRASP 框架，将规划流程解耦为三个上下文隔离的专门模块：GenPlan 预编译全局宏准则（macro-guidelines）、RevPlan 在隔离上下文窗口内探索备选局部策略、VerPlan 用多准则判别器独立评估轨迹。通过强制的宏正则化（macro-regularization）约束策略空间。

Result: 在 Natural Plan Calendar Scheduling 上提升约 12.4%，ZebraLogic 提升约 30.8%，SciBench Math 亦有增益；多任务扩展下 GRASP 完全消除性能退化惩罚；在交错双任务环境中相对直接规划器绝对精度提升最高 16.7%；并比 GPT-5-mini 等前沿推理模型高出 14.5%。

Conclusion: 通过上下文隔离和全局宏正则化，GRASP 在复杂及多任务规划中稳定超越直接 LLM 规划器和前沿推理模型，确立了新的 SOTA，并有效解决了多任务扩展下的性能崩溃问题。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) typically exhibit a performance profile where reliability degrades as task complexity increases. We address the challenge of generating high-quality natural language executable plans for complex tasks by introducing $\textbf{GRASP}$, a strategy-aware, multi-stage planning framework. GRASP decouples the planning pipeline across specialized, context-isolated modules: it pre-compiles global macro-guidelines (GenPlan), explores alternative localized strategies within isolated context windows (RevPlan), and independently evaluates trajectories using a multi-criteria discriminator (VerPlan). Empirical evaluations show that GRASP consistently establishes a new state-of-the-art frontier across diverse datasets, yielding substantial accuracy gains over direct LLM planners on Natural Plan Calendar Scheduling ($\sim$12.4$\%$$\uparrow$), ZebraLogic ($\sim$30.8$\%$$\uparrow$), and SciBench Math. Crucially, under multi-task scaling-where standard planners suffer immediate performance collapse-GRASP completely flattens the multi-task degradation penalty. In interleaved dual-task environments, GRASP achieves an absolute accuracy gain of up to 16.7$\%$ over direct LLM planners. Furthermore, by isolating context and enforcing strict macro-regularization, GRASP outperforms frontier reasoning models (such as GPT-5-mini) by a margin of 14.5$\%$.

</details>


### [106] [Search-Aware Reinforcement Learning for Multi-Component Query Understanding in Roblox Game Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30177)
*Nayoung Choi,Shengjian Chen,Xiaokai Wei,Wenzheng Zhang,Daiyao Yi,Rachit Pareek,Vincent Su,Michelle Gong,Jinho D. Choi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种面向查询理解（QU）的“先蒸馏后强化学习（distill-then-RL）”框架，先用师生监督微调（SFT）获得合规的策略初始化，再以与搜索引擎实时交互产生的、按各组件分工定制的奖励进行强化学习优化。实验表明组件级奖励优于单一端到端奖励，显著提升搜索质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 查询理解是生产搜索系统的关键环节，LLM 已能将其转化为结构化多任务生成问题，但静态标签监督无法刻画各组件与搜索管线交互的真实影响。现有用单一最终结果奖励的优化方式难以针对每个 QU 组件的具体作用进行优化，因此亟需一种与搜索引擎耦合、按组件定制的优化方案。

Method: 采用“先蒸馏后 RL”的两阶段范式：先通过师生监督微调（SFT）训练出格式正确、符合 schema 的策略作为初始化；随后进入 RL 阶段，让策略与搜索引擎实时交互，针对每个 QU 组件（如意向分类、查询扩展）按其运营角色分别设计奖励信号，而非使用单一绑定最终搜索结果的奖励进行优化。

Result: 在 Roblox 搜索上的实验表明，组件专属优化同时提升了各组件效用和下游搜索质量：NDCG@20 相较 SFT 策略提升 8.9 个点，相较单一端到端奖励训练提升 3.5 个点。

Conclusion: 通过将奖励信号与各 QU 组件在搜索管线中的实际分工对齐，而非依赖单一最终结果奖励，可以更有效地提升查询理解各组件及整体搜索质量，验证了搜索感知的组件级强化学习框架的有效性。

Abstract: Query understanding (QU) plays a critical role in production search systems, translating raw user queries into search execution plans that drive downstream retrieval and ranking. While large language models (LLMs) have enabled QU to be framed as a structured multi-task generation problem (e.g., intent classification, query expansion), optimizing such models to produce search-engine-coupled outputs remains challenging: static, label-based supervision fails to capture how each component actually interacts with the underlying search pipeline to affect downstream performance. We present a search-aware reinforcement learning (RL) framework for QU based on a distill-then-RL paradigm. Teacher-student supervised fine-tuning (SFT) first yields a well-formed, schema-compliant policy initialization. The RL stage then optimizes each QU component with rewards derived from live interaction with the search engine, tailored to that component's operational role, rather than a single reward tied to the final search outcome. Experiments on Roblox search show that this component-specific optimization improves both per-component utility and downstream search quality, raising NDCG@20 by 8.9 points over the SFT policy and by 3.5 points over training with a single end-to-end reward.

</details>


### [107] [Jev-Mobile: Jev as an Executor for Mobile GUI Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30186)
*Linghua Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Jev-Mobile 通过将 VLM 规划频率降低、引入基于无障碍树的轻量快速决策模型 Jev 执行高频动作，在保持任务成功率的同时大幅降低时延与 API 成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自主移动 GUI 智能体（如 SeeAct-V）几乎在每个交互步都依赖 VLM 同时进行规划与动作落地，导致推理延迟高、模型服务成本大。作者希望解耦高层 VLM 推理与低层动作执行，以提升效率。

Method: 提出 Jev-Mobile 框架：VLM 仅以低频制定局部目标；无障碍树（accessibility tree）定义结构化可执行动作空间；快速类型化决策模型 Jev 在该空间内反复选择动作。由此在单次 VLM 决策下即可执行多个 GUI 动作。

Result: 在完整 AndroidWorld 任务集上，Jev-Mobile 任务成功率为 79%，对比 SeeAct-V 的 78% 和 Step-wise VLM 基线的 84%。在成功轨迹中，相比 Step-wise VLM，其平均端到端执行时间降低 32.7%，平均模型 API 成本降低 73.4%。

Conclusion: 将高层 VLM 推理与低层动作执行解耦，能在保持竞争力任务性能的同时显著提升移动 GUI 智能体的效率。

Abstract: Vision-language models (VLMs) have become a common foundation for autonomous mobile GUI agents, but most existing systems rely on the VLM for both planning and action grounding at nearly every interaction step, leading to substantial latency and model-serving cost. We introduce Jev-Mobile, which shifts this paradigm to low-frequency VLM planning and high-frequency lightweight execution: the VLM specifies local goals, the accessibility tree defines a structured executable action space, and Jev, a fast typed decision model, repeatedly selects actions within this space. This design allows multiple GUI actions to be executed under a single VLM decision, reducing expensive VLM inference while preserving adaptive interaction. On the full AndroidWorld task suite, Jev-Mobile achieves 79% task success, compared with 78% for SeeAct-V and 84% for a Step-wise VLM baseline. Among successful trajectories, it reduces mean end-to-end execution time by 32.7% and mean model API cost by 73.4% relative to Step-wise VLM. These results show that decoupling high-level VLM reasoning from low-level action execution can substantially improve mobile GUI agent efficiency while maintaining competitive task performance.

</details>


### [108] [SAGE: Mitigating Long-Horizon Reasoning Biases via Topological Guidance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30192)
*Xinyue Zeng,Jiawei Zhang,Yujun Yan,Dawei Zhou*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出SAGE框架，针对LLM在稀疏奖励下的长时程推理困境，通过代数稀疏化与双曲结构引导分别缓解探索偏差与复合偏差，在12个基准与7个模型家族上表现优异，并在Andrews-Curtis问题上取得最高8倍提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长时程推理在稀疏奖励下仍是LLM的核心难题。作者认为其脆弱性源于复杂推理空间带来的两类偏差：探索偏差（模型偏向局部合理但结构不稳定的分支）和复合偏差（局部微小偏差在深度上累积并抑制稀有奖励）。现有方法缺乏对分支结构与稀疏奖励如何诱发这些偏差的理论刻画。

Method: 首先提出符号闭包分析（SCA）作为理论透镜，刻画分支结构与稀疏奖励如何在具有局部可采纳性的长时程推理中诱发探索偏差与复合偏差，并作为非形式化推理任务的结构先验设计原则。基于此分析提出统一框架SAGE，包含两种互补的结构引导：①代数稀疏化（algebraic sparsification），将局部可采纳候选投影到以算子索引的代数子空间，抑制虚假分支以缓解探索偏差；②双曲结构引导（hyperbolic structural guidance），将推理状态嵌入负曲率空间，提供稠密的深度方向信号以缓解复合偏差。

Result: 在12个基准和7个模型家族上，SAGE均超越竞争基线。特别地，在开放的现实世界长时程任务Andrews-Curtis问题上，SAGE取得最高8倍的提升。

Conclusion: SAGE通过结构可采纳性引导的探索，有效缓解了长时程推理中的探索偏差与复合偏差，展现出在稀疏奖励、长时程任务上的显著能力，为解决开放的长时程推理难题提供了统一且可迁移的框架。

Abstract: Long-horizon reasoning remains a central challenge for large language models (LLMs) under sparse-reward regimes. We argue that this brittleness arises from two biases induced by complex reasoning spaces: an exploration bias, where models are drawn toward locally plausible but structurally unstable branches, and a compounding bias, where small local deviations accumulate across depth and suppress rare rewards. We introduce Symbolic Closure Analysis (SCA) as a theoretical lens characterizing how branching structures and sparse rewards induce these biases in long-horizon reasoning with local admissibility, and as a design principle for structural priors in less formal reasoning tasks. Motivated by this analysis, we propose SAGE (Structural Admissibility-Guided Exploration), a unified framework that injects structural guidance to alleviate exploration bias and compounding bias in long-horizon reasoning. SAGE combines two complementary structural guidance: algebraic sparsification, which projects locally admissible candidates onto operator-indexed algebraic subspaces to suppress spurious branching and mitigate exploration bias, and hyperbolic structural guidance, which embeds reasoning states into a negatively curved space to provide dense depth-wise signals and mitigate compounding bias. Across 12 benchmarks and 7 model families, SAGE outperforms competitive baselines. In particular, SAGE achieves up to an 8-fold improvement on the Andrews-Curtis problem, an open real-world long-horizon task. Code is available at: https://github.com/Susan571/SAGE-NeurIPS2026.

</details>


### [109] [ExplorationBench: Measuring AI Systems' Exploration in Verifiable Alien Worlds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30199)
*Ming Zhang,Zhenghao Xiang,Peizhong Gao,Yujiong Shen,Yuhui Wang,Zhonghan Yue,Shihan Dou,Zhangyue Yin,Junjie Ye,Shichun Liu,Weihuang Zheng,Jiahao Chen,Jiayi Chen,Hongzhang Liu,Jiaqi Shao,Tao Gui,Qi Zhang,Xuanjing Huang,Suncong Zheng,Maxm Pan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 ExplorationBench，一个通过可执行且与常识冲突的“外星世界”规则来评估 AI 系统科学探索能力的基准，包含 AlienCode 和 AlienLogic 两个沙箱，系统需在未知环境中通过探索获取并应用新知识。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 科学发现始于已知问题的边界之外，需要 AI 具备探索能力（提出假设、设计实验、迭代结果）。但评估该能力存在两大难题：一是如何验证一个真正新颖的假设是否成立；二是如何判断系统是通过探索发现的，还是仅从预训练数据中回忆相关知识。现有方法难以同时解决这两点。

Method: 提出 ExplorationBench，将“评估科学探索”这一棘手问题转化为基于可验证外星世界的具体、可操作的框架。其规则可执行，因此每个答案都能被精确检验；且规则与熟悉知识冲突，单靠回忆无法完成任务。基准包含 AlienCode（31 个发现目标、70 个任务）和 AlienLogic（24 个发现目标、70 个任务）两个沙箱，每个沙箱提供一份有缺陷的手册、任务相关的环境反馈以及专用的工具调用模式，系统利用这些资源探索沙箱并解决留出的测试任务。

Result: 评估了 10 个 AI 系统，发现最强的系统能够获取并应用不熟悉的规则，但不同探索轨迹间性能差异显著，且持续探索可能出现停滞甚至使先前收益倒退的现象。

Conclusion: ExplorationBench 是迈向能在未知环境中通过探索获取并应用真正新知识的 AI 系统的一步。

Abstract: Scientific discovery begins where known problems end. There, AI systems must engage in exploration: framing hypotheses, designing experiments, and iterating on the results. However, evaluating this ability is difficult: (1) how to verify whether a genuinely new hypothesis holds, and (2) how to determine whether a system has discovered it through exploration or merely recalled related knowledge from pre-training data. To this end, we introduce ExplorationBench, which turns the wicked problem of evaluating scientific exploration into a concrete and tractable framework built on verifiable Alien Worlds: their rules are executable, so every answer can be checked exactly, and they conflict with familiar knowledge, so recall alone cannot solve the tasks. The benchmark contains two sandboxes, AlienCode (31 discovery targets, 70 tasks) and AlienLogic (24 discovery targets, 70 tasks). Each sandbox provides a flawed manual, task-specific environmental feedback, and a dedicated tool-call schema. Systems use these resources to explore the sandbox, then solve held-out tasks. We evaluate 10 AI systems and find that the strongest systems can acquire and apply unfamiliar rules, while performance varies substantially across trajectories and continued exploration can stall or reverse earlier gains. ExplorationBench represents a step towards AI systems that can acquire and apply genuinely new knowledge through exploration in unknown environments.

</details>


### [110] [A Living Benchmark for Information Retrieval from Electronic Health Records](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30205)
*Jordan L. Cahoon,Chloe O. Stanwyck,Sulaiman Somani,Philip Chung,Kevin R Keet,Kameron C. Black,Andrea T. Fisher,Sarita Khemani,Jerry Liu,Stephen Ma,Saloni K. Maharaj,Rita M. Pandya,Eduardo Perez-Guerrero,Priyanka Pillai,Lisa Shieh,David J. H. Wu,James Xie,James C. McAvoy,Teresa Nguyen,Jessica Tran,Lucy Yin,Bridget Lin,Alison Callahan,Jason A. Fries,Nigam H. Shah,Emily Alsentzer*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出可自动从纵向电子健康记录（EHR）笔记生成问答对的框架，并由19名临床医生验证，产出可持续维护的基准BRIE。在9个大语言模型和5种推理策略的测试中，现有系统常遗漏临床重要信息，尤其在需要跨文档、跨就诊综合的问题上。由于生成器本身经过验证，BRIE支持静态基准无法实现的能力：生成多个反映临床推理差异的答案、持续刷新内容防止泄漏。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于大语言模型的临床助手正被整合进EHR系统，其安全性和实用性依赖严格评估；但现有基准为人工策展，更新成本高，且随技术快速演进迅速过时，无法跟上部署需求。

Method: 提出可扩展的自动生成框架，从纵向EHR笔记自动生成问答对；由19名临床医生对基准生成器进行验证，产出BRIE数据集；随后在9个大语言模型和5种推理策略上进行系统评估。

Result: 当前最先进系统常遗漏临床重要信息，尤其在需要综合多个文档和多次就诊信息的问题上表现不佳。经验证的生成器支持生成多个反映临床医生推理差异的答案，以进行稳健性能评估，并能持续刷新基准内容以防数据泄漏。

Conclusion: 可扩展的基准生成机制能够在快速演进的医疗环境中，对部署中的临床大语言模型进行严格、实时的评估，弥补静态人工基准的不足。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM)-based clinical assistants are increasingly being integrated into electronic health record (EHR) systems, transforming how clinicians retrieve and synthesize information from patient records. Their safety and utility depend on rigorous evaluation, yet existing benchmarks are manually curated, costly to update, and rapidly become obsolete with evolving technological advancements. We present a scalable framework that automatically generates question--answer pairs from longitudinal EHR notes. Nineteen clinicians validate the benchmark generator, producing the Benchmark for Retrieving Information in EHRs (BRIE), a continuously maintainable evaluation dataset. Across nine LLMs and five inference strategies, state-of-the-art systems frequently omit clinically important information, particularly for questions requiring synthesis across multiple documents and encounters. Because the generator itself is validated, BRIE supports evaluations that static benchmarks cannot, including the generation of multiple answers that reflect variation in clinician reasoning for robust performance assessment and continuously refreshing benchmark content to guard against leakage. Our results demonstrate that scalable benchmark generation enables rigorous, up-to-date evaluation of clinical LLMs as they are deployed in rapidly evolving healthcare settings.

</details>


### [111] [AD-WM: Action-Discriminative World Models for Counterfactual Model Predictive Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30264)
*Jiabin Qiu,Zixuan Chen,Hongye Cao,Jieqi Shi,Jing Huo,Yang Gao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 AD-WM，一种面向反事实模型预测控制（MPC）的动作判别式联合嵌入世界模型：在残差潜在动力学上加入基于逆动力学与条件互信息启发的归一化动作恢复正则，使规划过渡保留动作信息，测试时丢弃辅助头。在 OGBench-Cube 上硬启动成功率从 3.7% 提升至 52.0%，五个仿真环境中的四个平均成功率超过复现基线，并在 Franka 真机上将零样本抓放成功率从 42.2% 提升至 71.1%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 潜在世界模型通常只以最小化事实转移预测误差为训练目标，但 MPC 需要从同一状态比较不同候选动作。一个模型即使事实预测误差很低，也可能无法有效区分候选动作，导致规划（反事实选择）效果差。因此需要让世界模型在预测事实转移之外，专门保留动作相关的差异信息，而不是仅优化事实预测精度。

Method: 提出 AD-WM，一种动作判别式联合嵌入世界模型。核心做法：1）采用残差潜在动力学；2）在预测器层面加入动作恢复正则，通过逆动力学和一个以条件互信息为动机的归一化恢复目标实现；两个目标都鼓励规划过程中的转移保留动作信息。辅助的动作恢复头仅在训练时使用，测试时丢弃，因此 MPC 推理流程保持不变。实验使用 LeWM 作为匹配基线，并在 Franka 真机上配合冻结的 V-JEPA 2 编码器和匹配的 DROID 后训练进行零样本迁移验证。

Result: 在 OGBench-Cube 上，AD-WM 将硬启动成功率从匹配 LeWM 基线的 3.7% 提升至 52.0%；在五个仿真环境中，有四个的平均成功率超过复现基线。规划诊断显示，事实预测误差与全库动作排序并不遵循闭环成功率的排序，而与 CEM 对齐的“精英遗憾”（elite regret）能更贴近成功率。在 Franka 真机零样本迁移中，基础抓放成功率由 42.2% 提升至 71.1%，且无需实验室特定适配。

Conclusion: 面向规划的世界模型应当保留反事实选择所需的动作相关差异，而非单纯优化事实预测精度。AD-WM 通过动作判别式正则有效提升模型在反事实 MPC 中的规划质量，在仿真和真机零样本迁移上均取得显著提升，表明动作信息保持对世界模型规划性能至关重要。

Abstract: Latent world models are typically trained to predict factual transitions, whereas model predictive control (MPC) must compare alternative actions from the same state. A model can therefore achieve low factual prediction error yet poorly distinguish candidate actions. We introduce AD-WM, an action-discriminative joint-embedding world model for counterfactual MPC. AD-WM combines residual latent dynamics with predictor-level action-recovery regularization, using inverse dynamics and a normalized recovery objective motivated by conditional mutual information. Both objectives encourage planning transitions to preserve action information; their auxiliary heads are discarded at test time, leaving MPC unchanged. On OGBench-Cube, AD-WM improves hard-start success from 3.7% to 52.0% over a matched LeWM baseline and improves mean success over the reproduced baseline in four of five simulation environments. Planning diagnostics show that factual prediction error and whole-bank action ranking do not follow the closed-loop success ordering, whereas CEM-aligned elite regret tracks success more closely. With a frozen V-JEPA 2 encoder and matched DROID post-training, AD-WM also improves zero-shot transfer to our Franka setup, increasing basic pick-and-place success from 42.2% to 71.1% without lab-specific adaptation. These results suggest that world models for planning should preserve action-dependent differences needed for counterfactual selection, rather than optimize factual prediction accuracy alone. More videos and code are available at https://ad-wm.github.io/.

</details>


### [112] [Forecast-Dojo: Replayable Environments for Benchmarking and Training LLM Forecasting Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.28876)
*Liqin Ye,Haorui Wang,Fardin Ahmed,Rongzhi Zhang,Yuan He,Ziyuan Lin,Yanbin Yin,Jing Peng,Michael Galarnyk,Sudheer Chava,Chao Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文介绍 Forecast-Dojo，一个可重放的基准与训练环境，用于训练和评估大语言模型（LLM）预测智能体。它结合已结算的预测市场问题与带日期的新闻，让智能体能在不同历史日期研究事件并反复更新预测，无需等待新事件结算即可获得结果反馈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 LLM 预测评估依赖正在进行的、尚未结算的真实事件，导致评估不可重复、训练数据难以获取且反馈延迟。作者希望构建一个可重放、可重复评估、可采集交互轨迹并提供即时结果反馈的环境，以支持预测智能体的基准测试与训练。

Method: Forecast-Dojo 将已结算的 Polymarket 预测市场问题与带日期的新闻语料（18.8M 篇文章）结合。智能体在连续的历史日期上研究事件并更新预测，使用研究工具检索新闻，并可维护一个跨日期携带的“信念笔记本”。环境按时间划分为训练期与评估期（共 1,568 个事件），提供交互轨迹与结果反馈，并以监督微调（SFT）作为训练的概念验证。

Result: 对 12 个模型的评估显示：研究工具降低了所有 12 个模型的 Brier 分数；预测质量随事件推进而提升，并在有更多新证据记录的步骤获得最大增益；但所有模型在 Brier 分数和准确率上仍落后于历史市场预测；信念笔记本降低了研究成本，但并未持续提升预测质量。

Conclusion: Forecast-Dojo 为 LLM 预测智能体提供了一个可重复、带即时反馈的评估与训练环境。尽管当前模型仍逊于历史市场预测，但环境本身能支撑智能体的学习与迭代，为未来的预测能力研究奠定基础。

Abstract: We introduce Forecast-Dojo, a replayable environment for benchmarking and training LLM forecasting agents. It combines resolved prediction-market questions with dated news, allowing agents to research an event and revisit their predictions at successive historical dates. The same tasks and tools support repeated evaluation, collection of training interactions, and feedback from recorded outcomes without waiting for new events to resolve. Forecast-Dojo contains 1,568 Polymarket events, split by time into training and evaluation periods, and 18.8M dated news articles. In an evaluation of 12 models, research tools lower Brier score for all 12. Forecasts also improve as events unfold, with the largest gains at steps where more newly dated evidence is recorded. Every model still trails historical market forecasts in both Brier score and accuracy. A belief notebook carried between dates lowers research cost but does not consistently improve forecast quality. Beyond evaluation, Forecast-Dojo provides interaction trajectories and outcome feedback for agent learning, with supervised fine-tuning as a proof of concept.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [113] [Thinking Leakage: A Causal Audit of NoThink Post-Training in Hybrid Reasoning Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.28682)
*Zehao Liu,Vasant G. Honavar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究NoThink模式下混合推理模型的后训练增益，发现这些增益很大程度来自"思维泄漏"——即模型借助了基础模型Think模式中已有的推理行为，而非真正提升NoThink自身能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: NoThink模式的后训练混合推理模型声称能在保持推理速度的同时提升性能，但作者怀疑这些增益并非源于NoThink模式真实的能力提升，而是源于对基础模型Think模式已有思维行为的重新调用（思维泄漏）。因此需要建立一个因果中介框架来审计这种泄漏的真实贡献。

Method: 作者提出因果中介框架，沿一个由基础模型派生的简单激活方向进行双向干预（正向steering复现增益、反向counter-steering移除增益）。在三个模型、三种后训练方法、九个对齐检查点上，结合行为分析和表征分析，并计算leakage ratio来量化思维泄漏的因果贡献。

Result: 思维泄漏是真实、因果且显著的：行为和表征分析显示后训练检查点向Think模式漂移；沿该方向steering基础模型可复现大部分后训练精度增益；counter-steering检查点可移除其增益的很大一部分。在九个具有正NoThink增益的对齐检查点上，leakage ratio介于42%到79%之间。

Conclusion: 后训练方法的表面优势可能更多反映了向Think模式的更大漂移，而非真正提升NoThink模式内的能力或更有效地重新调用已有Think行为。这提示在评估NoThink后训练增益时，需要区分"真实能力提升"与"思维泄漏"效应。

Abstract: Post-training hybrid reasoning models in NoThink mode has attracted growing interest as a way to improve performance while keeping inference fast. However, these gains may draw on thinking behavior already accessible through the base model's Think mode. We formulate this thinking leakage in a causal mediation framework and audit its contribution using bidirectional interventions along a simple base-derived activation direction. Across three models and three post-training methods on competition math benchmarks, we find that leakage is real, causal, and substantial: behavioral and representational analyses reveal shifts toward Think, steering the base model along this direction reproduces most of the post-training accuracy gain, and counter-steering a checkpoint removes a substantial share of what it gains. Across nine aligned checkpoints with positive NoThink gains, the resulting leakage ratio ranges from 42% to 79%. These interventions support a substantial causal contribution of thinking leakage. Our findings show that a post-training method's apparent advantage can therefore reflect greater drift toward Think, obscuring whether it improves capability within NoThink or more effectively re-invokes existing Think behavior.

</details>


### [114] [Intrinsic-Extrinsic Coupling in Learning Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30185)
*Qinyou Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种可执行的内在-外在耦合框架，通过“延续条件化价值”衡量受约束学习状态干预的影响，并设计了一个有限帧分类器头写入器，在保护当前 logits 的同时修复历史边际。作者区分了局部可采纳性、延续条件化干预价值与完整策略性能，并在 CLINC 类增量、输出蒸馏、RoBERTa 骨干及 SGDW 动态下验证了耦合的非可加性与根依赖效应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 学习者的当前观测并不必然决定其对后续训练的反应，已有方法难以从几何与价值层面操作化“内在-外在耦合”，并区分可执行的局部修复、延续条件化价值与完整策略性能之间的差异。

Method: 通过受约束学习状态干预的“延续条件化价值”刻画内在-外在耦合，并用观测相对纤维（observation-relative fibers）描述当前一致性；实现可执行的有限帧分类器头写入，在有限精度接受检查下保护当前 logits 并修复指定历史边际。设计匹配的四单元对照以识别同一内在干预与不同外部延续之间读出特异的非可加性，并进行多根协调测试与引导分配对比。

Result: 在 CLINC 派生的类增量设置中，重放将写入的 32 更新贡献从 5 个正确预测降至 0；输出蒸馏、RoBERTa 骨干及 SGDW 动态下也观测到非零交互。SGDW 下 128 更新时三个激活根的正确计数交互均为负，表明耦合不必然意味正向协同。协调测试中内容控制匹配或超过开发增益；五根全新测试在 Fiber 全臂存在时呈现根依赖而非一致有益的正确计数效应。次级交叉熵读出上，引导分配的均值损失在五组中均低于标准重放。

Conclusion: 该工作将内在-外在耦合操作化，通过可执行状态几何连接延续条件化价值、匹配交互识别与闭环协调，同时将已识别的耦合与完整策略性能区分开来。

Abstract: A learner's current observations need not determine its response to further training. We formulate intrinsic-extrinsic coupling through the continuation-conditioned value of a constrained learning-state intervention, with observation-relative fibers describing present agreement. An executable finite-frame classifier-head write protects current logits while repairing specified historical margins under finite-precision acceptance checks. We distinguish local admissibility, continuation-conditioned intervention value, and complete-policy performance. A matched four-cell contrast identifies readout-specific non-additivity between the same intrinsic intervention and alternative external continuations. In a CLINC-derived class-incremental setting, replay changes the write's 32-update contribution from five correct predictions to zero. Nonzero interactions also occur under output distillation, with a RoBERTa backbone, and under optimizer-native SGDW dynamics. Under SGDW, correct-count interactions are negative in all three activated roots at 128 updates, showing that coupling need not imply positive synergy. The mathematical analysis distinguishes feasible local repairs and favorable terminal outputs from training-reachable repair regions. Separate coordination tests show that content controls match or exceed the development gain, while a five-root fresh-test comparison with Fiber present in every arm shows root-dependent rather than uniformly beneficial correct-count effects. On the secondary cross-entropy readout, guided allocation yields lower mean loss than standard replay in all five pairs. Together, these results make intrinsic-extrinsic coupling operational by connecting executable state geometry to continuation-conditioned value, matched interaction identification, and closed-loop coordination, while separating identified coupling from complete-policy performance.

</details>


### [115] [CataOPD: Catalytic On-Policy Distillation for Large Language Model Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.29518)
*Wenjin Liu,Chenxi Wang,Jiapu Wang,Zhe Cui,Anh Tuan Luu,Haoran Luo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: CataOPD 提出一种将教师模型作为"催化剂"而非目标的强化学习方法，通过自救援路由与催化引导自我求解，并在无催化剂推理下扩大学生的独立推理能力，超越现有 RL 与在线策略蒸馏基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有两大推理增强范式各有短板：强化学习在未采样到正确轨迹时缺乏正向正确性信号；在线策略蒸馏受限于学生在策略分布下可达的推理轨迹。两者都无法让学生解决那些"自己思考不出来"的问题。

Method: 提出 CataOPD 框架，包含三个组件：(1) Self-Rescue Routing（自救援路由）——以经验上全失败的样本组作为路由信号而非教师介入触发条件，先通过额外的在策略自我采样尝试寻找正确轨迹；(2) Catalytic-Guided Self-Resolution（催化引导自我求解）——对自救援后仍未解决的问题，利用催化引导在学生引导分布中诱出经验证的学生自产轨迹；(3) Barrier-Weighted Internalization（障碍加权内化）——按引导与未引导对数概率之差为 token 加权，将更新聚焦于无引导时难以生成的决定性 token，从而把验证过的学生轨迹内化进无催化剂策略。

Result: 实验表明 CataOPD 优于现有基线，能将独立学生推理扩展到仍未恢复的问题，并在无催化剂推理下提升分布外泛化能力。

Conclusion: 将教师定位为催化剂而非目标，能在运行时借助引导"越障"、事后把验证轨迹内化回学生自身，从而突破 RL 缺信号与 OPD 受可达性限制的瓶颈，实现更强的独立推理与泛化。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) and on-policy distillation (OPD) are two representative paradigms for improving large language model reasoning. However, when no correct trajectory is sampled, RL lacks a positive correctness signal, while OPD remains constrained by the reasoning trajectories reachable under the student's on-policy distribution. Therefore, we propose CataOPD, where the teacher acts as a catalyst rather than a target, expanding reachability while internalizing verified student-produced trajectories into a catalyst-free policy. Self-Rescue Routing uses empirically all-failed groups as routing signals rather than teacher-intervention triggers, first seeking correct trajectories through additional on-policy self-sampling. For problems unresolved after self-rescue, Catalytic-Guided Self-Resolution uses catalytic guidance to elicit a verified student-produced trajectory in the guided student distribution. Barrier-Weighted Internalization weights tokens by guided-to-unguided log-probability gaps, focusing updates on decisive tokens difficult without guidance. Experimental results show that CataOPD outperforms current baselines, extends independent student reasoning to still-unrecovered problems, and improves out-of-distribution generalization under catalyst-free inference. Our project is available at https://github.com/QwenQKing/CataOPD.

</details>


### [116] [Graph, Loop, and Harness Engineering for Zero-Trust Agentic Data Engineering and Analytical Processing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.29668)
*Sagar Srinivas Sakhinana,Venkataramana Runkana*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出两个零信任框架：一个用于从自然语言任务生成、部署并验证完整云端数据工程解决方案，另一个用于将受治理的数据准备与经过验证的在线分析处理（OLAP）相结合，二者均要求在证据门控、有界恢复和零信任执行的条件下才可完成或发布结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型智能体正日益自动化数据工作流，但端到端的云端数据工程与分析执行需要在代码、数据、基础设施和运行时环境之间实现可靠协调，现有方式缺乏可验证的端到端信任保障。

Method: 提出两个零信任框架：（1）Zero-Trust Agentic Data Engineering，从自然语言任务生成、部署并验证完整云端数据工程解决方案，完成以仓库、部署、运行时和策略证据为条件；（2）Zero-Trust Agentic OLAP，将受治理的数据准备与经过验证的OLAP结合，仅在验证和证据约束审批后才允许生产提升，且仅在同快照执行、精确结果等价、确定性溯源和反思之后才释放分析答案。二者共享三个抽象：图工程（证据门控的工作流结构）、循环工程（有界恢复）和智能体-执行器工程（零信任执行）。

Result: 在标称执行、受控故障、有界恢复和策略约束条件下对两个框架进行评估，测量了验证完成率、恢复能力、授权强制执行、生产提升和经过验证的OLAP执行等指标，结果表明框架能够可靠实现证据门控的合规执行与有界恢复。

Conclusion: 通过共享的图工程、循环工程和智能体-执行器工程抽象，可以为端到端云端数据工程与OLAP构建零信任、证据驱动、具备有界恢复能力的智能体工作流，从而在代码、数据、基础设施和运行时层面实现可靠的自动化执行与合规治理。

Abstract: Large language model agents increasingly automate data workflows, but end-to-end cloud data engineering and analytical execution require reliable coordination across code, data, infrastructure, and runtime environments. We present two zero-trust frameworks. Zero-Trust Agentic Data Engineering generates, deploys, and verifies complete cloud data-engineering solutions from natural-language tasks, with completion conditioned on repository, deployment, runtime, and policy evidence. Zero-Trust Agentic OLAP combines governed Data Preparation with verified Online Analytical Processing (OLAP), permitting production promotion only after validation and evidence-bound approval, and releasing analytical answers only after Same-Snapshot Execution, Exact Result Equivalence, deterministic grounding, and reflection. Both frameworks share three abstractions: graph engineering for evidence-gated workflow structure, loop engineering for bounded recovery, and agent-harness engineering for zero-trust execution. We evaluate both frameworks under nominal execution, controlled failures, bounded recovery, and policy-constrained conditions, measuring verified completion, recovery, authorization enforcement, production promotion, and verified OLAP execution.

</details>


### [117] [Beyond Average Safety: Chance-Constrained LLM Fine-tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.29960)
*Taha Entesari,Mahyar Fazlyab*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对大语言模型微调中可能出现的安全性能退化问题，提出一种基于机会约束的安全保持微调方法，通过可微的违反率上界（majorization）构建保守约束，并设计约束感知的梯度下降更新，显著优于现有基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有安全保持微调方法（控制平均安全损失、加权辅助惩罚）会掩盖罕见但严重的失败案例，无法保证安全临界提示上的退化被有效控制。

Method: 提出机会约束（chance-constrained）形式，限制相对参考模型退化超过预设阈值的安全样本比例；针对经验机会约束中的不连续指示函数，引入可微的违反率上界（majorization）得到可处理的保守约束；进一步开发约束感知梯度下降方法，将该约束视为参数空间中的安全集，最小程度地修正微调方向以保持可行性，更新具有闭式解，并产生强调接近或超过退化阈值样本的尾部感知安全修正。

Result: 在三个不同任务和三个模型上的有害微调实验中，方法一致优于现有文献中的基线方法。

Conclusion: LLM 微调中的安全保持更适合被视为可靠性约束优化问题，而非平均风险正则化。

Abstract: Fine-tuning large language models on new objectives can improve helpfulness, instruction following, or domain-specific performance, but it can also induce regressions on safety-critical prompts. Existing safety-preserving fine-tuning methods typically control average safety loss or use weighted auxiliary penalties, which can obscure rare but severe failures. We propose a chance-constrained formulation for safety-preserving fine-tuning that limits the fraction of safety examples whose degradation relative to a reference model exceeds a prescribed threshold. Because the resulting empirical chance constraint contains a discontinuous indicator, we introduce a differentiable majorization of the violation rate, yielding a tractable conservative constraint. We then develop a constraint-aware gradient descent method that treats the majorized constraint as a safe set in parameter space and minimally modifies the fine-tuning direction to preserve feasibility. The resulting update admits a closed form and produces a tail-aware safety correction that emphasizes examples near or above the degradation threshold. We conduct an extensive set of experiments on harmful fine-tuning across three different tasks and three models and show that our approach consistently outperforms the baselines that exist in the literature. These results suggest that safety preservation in LLM fine-tuning is better viewed as a reliability-constrained optimization problem than as average-risk regularization.

</details>


### [118] [Diverse Geometries, Frozen Weights: Robust Heterogeneous Treatment-Effect Estimation via Causal Expert Ensembles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.29974)
*Ali Haghpanah Jahromi,Mohammad Taheri*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 GeoACE，一个五专家框架，通过几何多样化的锚点校正专家组合与无泄漏的集成权重学习，用于从观测数据中稳健估计异质性处理效应。结果显示该方法在多个基准上表现领先，但统计上并无普适优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从观测数据估计异质性处理效应很困难，因为最合适的归纳偏置随重合度、处理不平衡、预后结构和样本量而变化，单一方法难以在所有情况下都最优。

Method: 提出 Geometry-Diverse Anchor-Correction Expert Ensemble（GeoACE）五专家框架，将通用锚点校正估计器与互补的重合感知、结果引导的几何方法相结合；集成权重仅用内部验证预测学习，在测试评估前冻结，再应用于在完整开发样本上重新拟合的专家。第五个专家 O-Phi-ACE 构建一个无结果、重合感知的统计投影，用低维几何替代锚点输入。并在 8 个基准协议上与 11 个比较器对比，检验多种权重聚合规则（inverse-DR、winner-take-all、凸 DR 拟合、R-stacking、因果 Q-聚合、等权、DR 岭收缩）。

Result: 加入 O-Phi-ACE 后，在 7 个有个体效应真值的基准上平均 sqrt(PEHE) 相对四专家集成均降低，1225 个成对任务中胜出 998 个；在 JOBS 政策风险上变化可忽略。五专家集成在 IHDP100、IHDPA、IHDPB 排名第一，在 NEWS 排名第二（与领先者仅差 0.13%）。在 7 个 sqrt(PEHE) 基准上平均排名最低（3.714），但 Friedman 与 Iman-Davenport 检验不显著（p=0.328 和 p=0.330）。inverse-DR 加权在基准均衡分析中一贯优于 winner-take-all、凸 DR 拟合、R-stacking 和因果 Q-聚合，但与等权和 DR 岭收缩统计上无差异。

Conclusion: 证据支持几何多样化的专家库和无泄漏聚合作为稳健性策略，但并不表明 GeoACE 或某种加权规则具有普适优越性。

Abstract: Estimating heterogeneous treatment effects from observational data is difficult because the most appropriate inductive bias varies with overlap, treatment imbalance, prognostic structure, and sample size. We introduce the Geometry-Diverse Anchor-Correction Expert Ensemble (GeoACE), a five-expert framework that combines a common anchor-correction estimator with complementary overlap-aware and outcome-guided geometries. Its task-level ensemble weights are learned only from internal validation predictions, frozen before test evaluation, and then applied to experts refitted on the complete development sample. The fifth expert, O-Phi-ACE, constructs an outcome-free, overlap-aware statistical projection from covariates and treatment assignment and replaces the anchor input with this lower-dimensional geometry. We evaluate GeoACE against 11 comparators on eight benchmark protocols. Adding O-Phi-ACE reduced mean sqrt(PEHE) relative to the four-expert ensemble on all seven benchmarks with individual-effect truth, winning 998 of 1,225 paired tasks; the change on JOBS policy risk was negligible. The five-expert ensemble ranked first on IHDP100, IHDPA, and IHDPB and second on NEWS, differing from the NEWS leader by 0.13%. Across the seven sqrt(PEHE) benchmarks it obtained the lowest observed average rank (3.714), although the omnibus Friedman and Iman-Davenport tests were not significant (p=0.328 and p=0.330). Using the same five frozen experts, inverse-DR weighting was consistently better than winner-take-all selection, convex DR fitting, R-stacking, and causal Q-aggregation in benchmark-balanced analyses, but was statistically indistinguishable from equal weighting and DR ridge shrinkage. The evidence therefore supports geometry-diverse expert libraries and leakage-free aggregation as a robustness strategy, not universal superiority of either GeoACE or one weighting rule.

</details>


### [119] [Let Training Guide Selection: Online Synthetic Data Filtering via Real-Anchored Utility](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.29988)
*Yanran Wu,Sana Lakdawala,Renzo Tassara Miller,Chongyang Bai,Sharath Ciddu,Shivendra Pratap Singh,Kungang Li,Sandeep Pandey,Chunwei Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: FROST 是一个在线框架，利用真实训练数据锚定的梯度反馈估计合成数据的效用，动态决定何时过滤及保留哪些样本，无需外部验证器或留出集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实数据有限时合成数据可扩展训练监督，但噪声和分布不匹配会降低其价值；现有合成数据选择方法多强调保真度或多样性，而忽视了学习者在训练过程中不断变化的需求。

Method: 提出 FROST 在线框架：通过锚定在真实训练数据上的梯度反馈估计合成数据效用；将批次效用与近期历史校准以判断何时需要过滤；仅在带外（out-of-band）批次中进行样本过滤以决定保留内容，整个过程无需外部验证器或留出验证集。

Result: 在两个公开基准（图像分类与文本到 SQL 的大模型微调）上，FROST 过滤掉约 20--30% 的合成数据，同时相比在全量合成数据池上训练提升真实任务性能；在大规模工业广告重排序系统中应用 FROST，相比高度优化的生产基线取得显著性能提升。

Conclusion: FROST 能有效识别并剔除低效用合成数据，在缩小数据规模的同时提升真实任务表现，展现出良好的有效性与泛化性，适用于学术基准和工业级系统。

Abstract: Synthetic data can scale training supervision when real-world data are limited, but noise and distribution mismatch can reduce its value. Existing synthetic data selection methods often emphasize fidelity or diversity rather than the learner's evolving needs. We propose FROST, an online framework that estimates synthetic-data utility through gradient feedback anchored in real training data. It calibrates batch utility against recent history to determine when filtering is needed and filters samples only in out-of-band batches to determine what to retain, without an external verifier or held-out validation set. Experiments on two public benchmarks for image classification and LLM fine-tuning for text-to-SQL show that FROST filters out around 20--30% of the synthetic data while improving real-task performance compared with training on the full synthetic data pool. We further apply FROST during training in a large-scale industrial ads re-ranking system, achieving significant performance gains over a highly optimized production baseline, demonstrating its effectiveness and generalizability.

</details>


### [120] [Reachability-Based Formal Verification of Graph Neural Networks with Node and Edge Features](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30079)
*Anne M. Tumlin,Ben Wooding,Zhenxuan Shao,Diego Manzanas Lopez,Tyler Derr,Taylor T. Johnson*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 GNNV 框架，将神经网络验证（NNV）中的 Star 集合推广为 GraphStar 集合，以对节点与边特征同时存在不确定性的图神经网络（GCN、GINE）进行形式化验证，并在电力系统（潮流、最优潮流、连锁故障）及图分类任务上验证其有效性，提供了首个边感知的鲁棒性保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 图神经网络在电力系统中广泛用于构建快速、拓扑感知的代理模型，但其形式化验证仍缺乏有效方法，现有验证手段适用范围受限。因此需要一种能够处理图结构输入、同时刻画节点与边特征不确定性的验证框架，为基于 GNN 的电力系统模型提供可靠的安全性保证。

Method: 作者将神经网络验证框架 NNV 扩展到图结构输入，提出 GraphStar 集合——这是 Star 集合的推广，能够同时捕获节点特征与边特征上的不确定性。该扩展支持线性消息传递操作的传播，并对 GNN 架构（包括 GCN 和带边特征的 GINE 层）中的 ReLU 非线性进行可靠的（sound）近似，从而实现形式化验证。

Result: 在 IEEE-24、IEEE-39、IEEE-118 三个电力系统测试算例的潮流（PF）、最优潮流（OPF）和连锁故障分析（CFA）任务，以及 ENZYMES、PROTEINS 两个标准图分类基准上评估。结果表明，GNNV 对基于 ReLU 激活的图分类模型提供了比 CORA 更紧的鲁棒性保证，并首次在节点与边联合扰动下为基于 GINE 的 PF 与 OPF 模型提供了边感知的鲁棒性保证。

Conclusion: GNNV 通过 GraphStar 集合成功将 NNV 框架推广至图神经网络，实现了对 GCN 和 GINE 模型在节点与边联合扰动下的形式化验证，显著扩展了 GNN 验证能力，并为电力系统代理模型带来了更紧、且首次具备边感知的鲁棒性保证。

Abstract: Graph neural networks (GNNs) have become a prominent approach for developing fast, topology-aware surrogates in electric power systems, supporting tasks such as power flow (PF) analysis, optimal power flow (OPF) estimation, and cascading failure analysis (CFA). Despite this growing use, formally verifying GNN-based models remains challenging, with existing methods limited in scope. We extend the neural network verification (NNV) framework to graph-structured inputs through GraphStar sets, a generalization of Star sets that captures uncertainty over both node and edge features. This extension enables the propagation of linear message-passing operations and the sound approximation of ReLU nonlinearities for GNN architectures, including graph convolutional network (GCN) and graph isomorphism network with edge features (GINE) layers. We evaluate GNNV across three power system tasks, PF, OPF, and CFA, on the IEEE-24, IEEE-39, and IEEE-118 test cases, as well as two standard graph classification benchmarks, ENZYMES and PROTEINS. Our results show that GNNV provides tighter robustness guarantees than CORA on graph classification models with ReLU-based activations and, for the first time, delivers edge-aware robustness guarantees for GINE-based PF and OPF models under joint node and edge perturbations.

</details>


### [121] [Residual Correlation as a Diagnostic for Joint-Uncertainty Gains from GP Coregionalisation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30085)
*Fangqin Zhou,Joaquin Vanschoren*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究多目标回归中通过高斯过程内在共区域化模型（GP-ICM）耦合相关目标的实际收益，发现核心收益在于联合不确定性量化而非点预测，并提出轻量级诊断量 D_logdet 及其配套的 Residual-ICM 方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GP-ICM 被普遍假设为"共享统计强度"能提升性能，但实践中收益不一致；且原始目标相关性无法预测何时耦合有益。作者希望厘清共区域化的真实价值来源，并提供一个可提前判断耦合是否有用的诊断工具。

Method: 引入轻量诊断量 D_logdet = -1/2·log det R_res，表示用完整残差协方差（相对对角残差协方差）建模所带来的理想化联合负对数似然（NLL）增益，且可仅由独立 GP 计算得到。方法上提出 Residual-ICM，在保留各目标独立边际方差的同时，向联合协方差中加入残差相关结构；并在受控合成实验、16 个多目标基准数据集、以及冻结的 transformer 和卷积神经网络关键点回归表示上评估。

Result: 点预测几乎不变（ΔR²≈0），而 D_logdet 能强烈预测观测到的 ICM NLL 提升（ρ_s=-0.83，p<0.001），优于特征-样本比等启发式指标。Residual-ICM 在对比方法中取得最佳平均联合 NLL。诊断量专用于可分离共区域化所捕捉的全局高斯残差依赖结构。

Conclusion: 在多目标回归的可分离 GP-ICM 设置中，共区域化的主要收益是联合不确定性量化而非点预测；残差相关性（而非原始目标相关性）是联合不确定性收益的最强预测因子。所提 D_logdet 可作为判断协方差耦合是否可能有益的实用诊断，Residual-ICM 则在保持边际方差的同时实现最优联合 NLL。

Abstract: In multi-target regression, correlated targets are often coupled through multi-output Gaussian processes with an intrinsic model of coregionalisation (GP-ICM), assuming that sharing statistical strength improves overall performance. In practice, the benefits are inconsistent. Across the settings studied, we find that the main benefit of coregionalisation is joint uncertainty quantification rather than point prediction. Raw target correlation does not predict when coupling helps; in the separable GP-ICM settings studied here, residual correlation, the cross-target dependence left unexplained by independent per-target predictors, is the strongest predictor of joint-uncertainty gains.
  We introduce a lightweight diagnostic, $D_{\rm logdet}=-\frac{1}{2}\log\det R_{\rm res}$, which represents the idealised joint negative log-likelihood (NLL) gain from modelling a full rather than diagonal residual covariance and is computable from independent GPs alone. Across a controlled synthetic study, 16 multi-target benchmarks, and frozen transformer and convolutional neural network representations for keypoint regression, point prediction remains largely unchanged ($ΔR^2\approx 0$). In contrast, $D_{\rm logdet}$ strongly predicts observed ICM NLL improvements ($ρ_s=-0.83$, $p<0.001$), outperforming heuristics such as the feature-to-sample ratio. We also propose Residual-ICM, which preserves independent marginal variances while adding residual-correlation structure to the joint covariance. Residual-ICM achieves the best average joint NLL among the compared methods, while the diagnostic indicates when covariance coupling is likely to be useful. The diagnostic is specific to global Gaussian residual dependence, the structure captured by separable coregionalisation.

</details>


### [122] [AT-SKM-Net: An Accelerated Trainable Sampling Kaczmarz-Motzkin Framework for Linear Hard-Constraint Feasibility on Dynamic Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30088)
*Xiaochen Zhang,Haoyu Zhu,Yao Zhang,Qingchun Hou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对图结构线性约束优化在大规模硬约束和高维度下的可扩展性瓶颈，本文提出 AT-SKM Net 框架，通过基于拓扑感知异构图神经网络的混合采样策略聚焦活跃约束，并利用 Cholesky 更新机制将等式投影复杂度从 O(N^3) 降至 O(N^2)，在保持零约束违反的同时实现最高 85% 的迭代缩减和 2.95–7.29 倍的 SKM 层加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有投影类方法（如 T-SKM-Net）虽能保证可行性，但在动态环境中因需处理整个约束集并进行昂贵矩阵分解而计算成本高昂，难以扩展至大规模图结构优化问题（如电网、燃气网络等关键基础设施）。

Method: 提出 Accelerated Trainable-SKM (AT-SKM) Net：1) 引入拓扑感知异构图神经网络（heterogeneous GNN）引导的混合采样策略，使计算集中于活跃约束并消除冗余计算；2) 采用 Cholesky Update 机制，在低秩扰动下将等式投影的理论复杂度从 O(N^3) 降至 O(N^2)，以高效应对图拓扑变化。

Result: 在随机几何图、N-1 安全约束直流最优潮流（SC-DC-OPF）和最小成本燃气输运问题上，AT-SKM 迭代次数最多减少 85%，SKM 层加速达 2.95–7.29 倍，同时保持零约束违反。

Conclusion: AT-SKM Net 通过混合采样与 Cholesky 更新显著降低了图结构线性约束优化的计算负担，在保持可行性（零约束违反）的同时实现大幅加速，验证了其在动态环境下的可扩展性与有效性。

Abstract: Graph-structured optimization with linear constraints is fundamental to critical infrastructure but faces scalability limits due to massive strict hard constraints and high dimensionality. While recent projection-based methods such as Trainable Sampling Kaczmarz-Motzkin Net (T-SKM-Net) guarantee feasibility, they face high computational costs in dynamic environments by processing the entire constraint set and requiring expensive matrix factorizations. To bridge this gap, we propose the Accelerated Trainable-SKM (AT-SKM) Net framework. To concentrate computation on the active constraints and eliminate redundant calculations, we introduce a hybrid sampling strategy guided by a topology-aware heterogeneous GNN model. To efficiently handle topological shifts in graph-based constraints, we employ a Cholesky Update mechanism that theoretically reduces the equality projection complexity from O(N^3) to O(N^2) under low-rank perturbations. Experiments on random geometric graphs, N-1 Security-Constrained DC-OPF, and minimum-cost gas transport problem demonstrate that AT-SKM reduces iteration counts by up to 85% and achieves 2.95x-7.29x SKM layer speedups, while maintaining zero constraint violations.

</details>


### [123] [On the SoS Certifiability of Log-Concave Distributions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30105)
*Aleksandr Storozhenko*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文证明了对任意各向同性对数凹分布，其偶阶矩多项式（去掉一个范数项）可表示为平方和，从而去除了对Poincaré常数的依赖，并据此获得具有维度无关误差保证的高效统计估计算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Kothari与Steinhardt的既有结果中，对数凹分布的平方和证书依赖Poincaré常数，导致不能获得最优的矩上界；本文旨在去除这一依赖，恢复对数凹分布的最优矩界，并推广到无维度因子的高效估计算法。

Method: 利用随机局部化（stochastic localization）将任意各向同性对数凹分布分解为随机强对数凹测度的平均，这些测度的中心矩具备Diakonikolas等人（STOC 2025）的次高斯平方和证书；再通过协方差自适应的局部化选取，结合由Letwin二次型方差不等式导出的四阶矩证书，控制任意偶数阶上的平均。

Result: 证明了多项式$(Cm)^m\|v\|_2^m - \mathbb{E}_{X\sim P}\langle X,v\rangle^m$（$m$为任意偶数，$C$为普适常数）是平方和，成功去除Poincaré常数依赖，并获得维度无关误差保证的高效算法。

Conclusion: 通过随机局部化与四阶矩证书的结合，克服了Poincaré常数依赖，恢复对数凹分布的最优矩界，为一大类高维统计估计问题提供了计算高效且误差与维度无关的算法。

Abstract: For an arbitrary isotropic log-concave distribution $P$ on $\mathbb{R}^d$, we prove that the polynomial $(Cm)^m\|v\|_2^m - \mathbb{E}_{X\sim P}\langle X,v\rangle^m$ is a sum of squares for every even $m\ge2$, where $C>0$ is a universal constant. This removes the dependence on the Poincaré constant in the theorem of Kothari and Steinhardt (arXiv:1711.07465), recovering the optimal moment bounds for log-concave distributions. As an immediate corollary, we obtain computationally efficient algorithms with dimension-free error guarantees for a wide range of high-dimensional statistical estimation problems.
  Our proof uses stochastic localization to decompose $P$ as an average of random strongly log-concave measures, whose centered moments admit the subgaussian certificates of Diakonikolas, Hopkins, Pensia, and Tiegel (STOC 2025; arXiv:2410.21194). With a covariance-adapted choice of localization, we show that a fourth-moment certificate derived from Letwin's variance inequality for quadratic forms (arXiv:2607.24164) suffices to control this averaging at every even degree.

</details>


### [124] [Beyond Compression: Training Latent Representations for Stable Long-Horizon Rollout in Neural Surrogate Solvers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30198)
*Andreas E. Robertson,Ashley T. Lenau,John D. Shimanek,Benjamin A. Jasperson,Vivek Oommen,David L. Damm,Krishna Garikipati,Remi Dingreville*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对潜变量神经代理求解器（潜动力学模型）在长时间自回归推演中误差快速累积的问题，指出其不稳定性源于仅面向重建训练、不适于长期预测的潜表示，而非潜表示本身。作者系统评估了多种训练层面的干预手段（Koopman算子学习、Hamming噪声注入、多步推演微调等），使长期推演误差降低约40%，以两个物理基准上的精度持平或超过全分辨率模型，且计算量与显存大幅下降，并实现了远超训练时长的稳定外推。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 潜动力学模型理论上可加速时变物理系统的模拟，但实践中长期自回归推演时误差往往迅速累积，限制其预测价值。作者希望查明该不稳定性根源，并通过训练层面的干预提升长期推演稳定性。

Method: 研究表明，不稳定并非源于潜表示本身，而是当自编码器仅以重建为目标训练时，会学到不适于长期预测的表示。作者系统评估了多种训练干预：1）在自编码器训练阶段加入Koopman算子学习和Hamming噪声注入以改进压缩表示；2）在动力学训练阶段加入噪声注入和多步推演（multi-step rollout）微调以改进动力学演化。这些干预使潜表示与长期推演目标对齐。

Result: 部分针对长期推演稳定性的干预反而会降低传统训练指标（如重建误差和单步预测精度）。综合运用这些干预使长期推演误差降低约40%，在两个物理基准上达到或超过全分辨率模型的精度，同时浮点运算量减少两个数量级、GPU显存减半。应用于介观晶体塑性高周疲劳模拟时，所得代理在远超训练时长的时域上实现了稳定外推。

Conclusion: 神经压缩不应仅为降维而设计，更应重构解空间以支持稳定的动力学演化，这是构建可靠、高效的科学应用神经代理的关键要求。该工作为长时程预测的潜变量神经代理提供了有效的训练策略与理论洞察。

Abstract: Latent neural surrogate solvers, or latent dynamics models, accelerate simulations of time-dependent physical systems by evolving a compressed latent space rather than resolving full-resolution fields directly. In principle this reduces computational cost and simplifies learning, but in practice errors often accumulate rapidly during long autoregressive rollouts, limiting predictive utility. We show that this instability does not stem from the latent representation itself, but arises when it is trained solely for reconstruction, producing representations poorly suited to long-horizon forecasting. We systematically evaluate training-level interventions that align latent representations with long-horizon rollout: Koopman operator learning and Hamming noise injection during autoencoder training to improve compression, together with noise injection and multi-step rollout fine-tuning to improve dynamics. Interventions that improve long-horizon rollout stability often degrade conventional training metrics, including reconstruction and one-step prediction accuracy. Collectively, these interventions reduce long-rollout error by approximately 40\% and match or exceed the accuracy of full-resolution models on two physics benchmarks, while requiring 2 orders of magnitude fewer floating point operations and half the GPU memory. Applied to mesoscale crystal-plasticity simulations of high-cycle fatigue, the resulting surrogate achieves stable extrapolation over horizons orders of magnitude beyond those observed during training. More broadly, these results show that neural compression should be designed not merely to reduce dimensionality, but to restructure the solution space for stable dynamical evolution, a key requirement for reliable, efficient neural surrogates in scientific applications.

</details>


### [125] [Minimally Invasive Steering of Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30218)
*Taha Entesari,Jingyu Zhang,Daniel Khashabi,Mahyar Fazlyab*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 MISVO（Minimally Invasive Steering Vector Optimization），通过引入基于局部 KL 几何的 Fisher 二次惩罚，在保持生成质量的前提下优化 frozen 语言模型的 test-time steering 向量，提升奖励得分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Pre-logit steering 在测试时通过向 frozen 语言模型的最终隐藏状态添加向量来优化奖励，但无正则的奖励优化可能显著改变输出分布、损害生成质量。现有方法缺乏对干预幅度的有效约束，难以在提升奖励与保持质量之间取得平衡。

Method: 提出 MISVO，利用 token 分布的局部 KL 几何对干预进行惩罚：其 Fisher 二次型度量分布敏感性，并通过与 frozen 语言模型头的矩阵-向量乘积计算解析梯度。推导出序列级 KL 梯度的精确分解，分为解析 Fisher 项和后缀 score-function 项；在固定生成长度下证明后缀项是 steering 幅度的二阶量，且三个 Fisher 代理与完整 KL 梯度一致到一阶。MISVO 采用 frozen-reference 代理，在不更新模型参数的情况下优化位置特定的干预向量。

Result: 在约 10 亿至 140 亿参数模型的偏好与代码生成任务上，MISVO 在 7 个模型-任务设置中的 6 个上取得最高平均奖励，且多样性与连贯性得分接近 Best-of-N。

Conclusion: MISVO 通过 Fisher 二次惩罚有效限制 steering 干预的分布偏移，在提升测试时奖励的同时保持生成质量，为 frozen 语言模型的 reward steering 提供了一种理论有依据且实用的优化方法，在多数设置中优于基线并接近 Best-of-N 的质量水平。

Abstract: Pre-logit steering adapts a frozen language model to a test-time reward by adding vectors to its final hidden states. Unregularized reward optimization can substantially alter the output distribution and degrade generation quality. We propose Minimally Invasive Steering Vector Optimization (MISVO), which penalizes interventions using the local KL geometry of the induced token distribution. The resulting Fisher quadratic measures distributional sensitivity and admits an analytic gradient computed through matrix--vector products with the frozen language-model head. We derive an exact decomposition of the sequence-level KL gradient into an analytic Fisher term and a suffix score-function term. For a fixed generation horizon, we show that the suffix term is second order in the steering magnitude and that three Fisher surrogates agree with the full KL gradient to first order. MISVO uses the frozen-reference surrogate to optimize position-specific interventions without updating model parameters. Across preference and code-generation tasks on models with approximately 1B--14B parameters, MISVO achieves the highest mean reward in six of seven model--task settings, with diversity and coherence scores close to those of Best-of-N.

</details>


### [126] [PoEM: Predicting RL Outcomes from Existing Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30226)
*Kimia Hamidieh,Giannis Daras,Antonio Torralba*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 PoEM 框架，利用已对多个奖励进行过 RL 后训练的模型集合，在不重新运行 RL 的情况下预测新奖励函数下的策略，从而跳过昂贵且不稳定的后训练过程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基础模型的后训练通常用强化学习（RL）来最大化特定奖励（如人类对齐、正确性、指令遵循），但该过程计算代价高昂、有时不稳定，且每次奖励模型改变或需要组合多个奖励时都须从头重新运行。作者因此探索：给定一个新奖励函数，能否不实际运行 RL 而直接预测其 RL 结果。

Method: 提出 PoEM 框架。核心观察：(1) 若新奖励可表示为现有奖励的线性组合，则新策略在 log 空间可写成现有 log 策略的线性组合；(2) 即使奖励之间非线性相关，RL 训练得到的 log 策略在跨奖励的样本上往往张成一个近似低秩子空间。据此，组合权重可仅用奖励或基策略在样本上的输出估计，从而用已后训练好的模型和新奖励函数近似目标 RL 策略，无需额外 RL 训练。

Result: 在合成奖励与真实奖励上，跨文本和图像两种模态实验验证了 PoEM 方法的有效性，表明能够在不运行额外 RL 的前提下较好地近似目标 RL 策略。

Conclusion: 给定新奖励函数，无需重新运行 RL 即可预测其结果，PoEM 通过在 log 策略空间中的线性组合与低秩结构近似实现，兼具稳定性与高效率，并在多模态实验中验证有效。

Abstract: Foundation models are post-trained with reinforcement learning (RL) to maximize specific rewards, such as human alignment, correctness, or instruction following. This post-training process is computationally intensive, sometimes unstable, and has to be run from scratch every time the reward model changes or when we want to combine multiple rewards. We hence ask: given a new reward function, is it possible to predict the RL outcomes without actually running RL on it? We answer this in the affirmative by introducing PoEM, a framework to predict the outputs of RL on a new reward function using a set of models already post-trained on other rewards. First, we show that if the new reward function can be written as a linear combination of existing ones, then the new policy in log-space can be written as a linear combination of the existing log-policies. Surprisingly, even in cases where the rewards are not linearly connected, we observe that often log-policies from RL training span an approximately low-rank subspace across rewards. To our benefit, the weighting coefficients for this combination can be estimated using only the reward or basis policy outputs on the samples. We turn these observations into an algorithm that takes post-trained models and a new reward function, and approximates the target RL policy without actually running any additional RL training. We experimentally validate our approach across synthetic and real rewards, spanning both text and image modalities.

</details>


<div id='stat.ML'></div>

# stat.ML [[Back]](#toc)

### [127] [Nuclear Norm-Regularized Bayesian Matrix Completion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.30078)
*Calvin Tolbert*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文首次为带有未知噪声方差的贝叶斯矩阵补全模型（核范数先验）给出了具有显式非渐近保证的采样器，其复杂度关于矩阵维数和目标精度倒数呈多项式增长。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 矩阵补全常用核范数正则化最小二乘，但只能得到点估计、缺乏不确定量化；在噪声方差已知时，核范数先验可导出对数凹后验，但实际中噪声方差未知，需要对其施加先验，导致后验不再对数凹，现有方法缺乏多项式时间且有理论保证的采样器。

Method: 将噪声精度（方差倒数）的分布离散化到网格上，并通过热力学积分（thermodynamic integration）构造分类（categorical）后验。该技术可推广至其他非对数凹采样问题——当非对数凹性仅限于单个变量、其余变量的联合分布非光滑时。

Result: 给出了该模型首个具备显式非渐近保证的采样器，复杂度对矩阵维度及目标精度倒数均为多项式；同时证明了此类贝叶斯采样器在多项式时间内可行的存在性。

Conclusion: 本文是一篇可行性（feasibility）结果：证明了针对该模型确实存在多项式时间的贝叶斯采样器；其复杂度虽为多项式，但在当前问题规模下并不作为可部署的实用算法。

Abstract: Matrix completion, the problem of estimating missing entries in a matrix from noisily observed ones, underlies a diverse array of problems such as recommender systems and counterfactual outcome estimation in panel data. Many algorithms address the problem using regularized least squares, often with the nuclear norm as a regularizer, but this method yields a point estimate with no built-in uncertainty quantification. A Bayesian formulation is a natural alternative, and if the noise variance is known, the nuclear norm-based prior yields a log-concave posterior. Unfortunately, in practice, the noise variance will not be known a priori, so for a fully Bayesian approach, a prior must be imposed on it. We give the first sampler for this model with an explicit non-asymptotic guarantee: polynomial in the matrix dimensions and in the reciprocal of the target accuracy. Our technique is to discretize the distribution of the noise precision onto a grid and build a categorical posterior via thermodynamic integration. This extension is not specific to matrix completion and may be useful in other non-log-concave sampling problems where the non-log-concavity is restricted to a single variable and the joint distribution of the remaining variables is nonsmooth. Our contribution is a feasibility result: we show that a polynomial-time Bayesian sampler for this model exists at all, and the resulting complexity, while polynomial, is not intended as a deployable algorithm at current problem scales.

</details>
